# Health and Care Act 2022, SCHEDULE 4

> Text as at 2025-07-07.
>
> Extent: England, Wales.

## SCHEDULE 4 — Integrated care system: minor and consequential amendments

*Extent: E+W*

Section 32

### 1. Reserve and Auxiliary Forces (Protection of Civil Interests) Act 1951[^c-ee9789174b9a588787c46d188fde8ac1][^c-b966affd0636b84ac2bf21d8ef4cfd88]

In Part 1 of Schedule 2 to the Reserve and Auxiliary Forces (Protection of Civil Interests) Act 1951 (capacities in respect of which payments under Part 5 of the Act may be made, and paying authorities), in paragraph 15—

- (a) in the first column (headed “capacity”), for “, a clinical commissioning group,” substitute “, an integrated care board,”;
- (b) in the second column (headed “paying authority”), for “, clinical commissioning group,” substitute “, integrated care board,”.

### 2. Public Records Act 1958[^c-431bcaf2473b23995d70bcc8a370a73c][^c-ec105e18b21f494c04709cef87a6bab1]

In Schedule 1 to the Public Records Act 1958 (bodies the records of which are public records), in Part 1 of the Table at the end of paragraph 3—

- (a) for “, clinical commissioning groups,” substitute “, integrated care boards,”;
- (b) for “, a clinical commissioning group”, in both places it occurs, substitute “, an integrated care board”;
- (c) for “paragraph 20 of Schedule 1A” substitute “paragraph 23 of Schedule 1B”.

### 3. Public Bodies (Admission to Meetings) Act 1960[^c-02765ec331406783e4cbe38bd1db5adf][^c-416cca5361b56703efd3810e1db362f4]

In the Schedule to the Public Bodies (Admission to Meetings) Act 1960 (public authorities to which that Act applies) in paragraph 1, after paragraph (o) insert—

> - (p) an integrated care board established under section 14Z25 of the National Health Service Act 2006;

.

**216.**[^c-01b1c5bbc1a0333050ad82464858aa44][^c-a62cfc018ad9a52e2bc2fe942761f6f8] In Schedule 5 (eligibility and regulation of local auditors), in paragraph 5, in the modified section 1214 of the Companies Act 2006—

- (a) in subsection (1), omit “(3),”;
- (b) for subsection (3) substitute—

  > (3) In relation to a relevant authority that is an integrated care board, subsection (2)(a) has effect as if “or officer” were omitted.

**217.**[^c-e09c1e2a0959daec3aa42f5dc5c8fba8][^c-acd1b1f38cba921cbc83e259dd3dec41] In Schedule 7 (reports and recommendations), in paragraph 4(8), for paragraph (b) substitute—

>   - (i) the group of people for whom it has core responsibility, and
>   - (ii) anyone who lives within its area but does not fall within sub-paragraph (i)

.
